## Completed 2026-06-20

- User-entered date updates are no longer accepted in the recent transaction update flow; the date field is reserved for reconciliation-origin automation that can use the bank transaction date.
- Recent-action update, move, delete, and undo paths now reopen linked reconciliation items by action-log ID so confirmed/matched bank items do not stay stale after the sheet row changes.
- Reconciliation action-link reopening supports grouped match IDs such as `id1+id2`.
- Normal unresolved reconciliation inbox/digest/session views now use a 60-day max transaction age through `BOOKIEBOT_RECONCILIATION_MAX_AGE_DAYS`.
- Old unresolved reconciliation records remain in storage, but they are excluded from normal unresolved review views unless lower-level/admin code asks without an age cutoff.
- Added regression tests for date rejection, reconciliation reopen hooks after recent-action mutations, grouped action-link reopening, and the 60-day unresolved-item cutoff.
- Existing-row reconciliation amount mismatches are resolved by the user's match confirmation: once the user chooses the matching row, BookieBot updates the sheet amount to the bank transaction amount and confirms the reconciliation item.
- Added regression coverage proving single-row mismatch confirmation updates the matched sheet/action row to the bank amount after the user selects the match.
- Recent-action move prompts now explain when an item name is needed because the destination category requires it.
- Pending move-item replies can now be canceled without using `cancel` as the item name.
- Recent-action move no longer asks users to supply missing dates manually; missing source-row dates produce a system/source-row correction message.
- Move category buttons now omit the transaction's current category and use source-category-aware prompt copy.
- Grouped reconciliation amount mismatches now offer a normal button-based adjustment path: choose one selected row to absorb the delta, update it to the bank-total-compatible amount, and confirm the group.
- Added regression coverage for grouped match adjustment, adjustment buttons, and updated mismatch guidance.
- One-word `recent` now routes directly to recent transactions instead of falling through to LLM parsing.
- Expense sheet access now retries once before failing, which protects normal expense logging from a transient Google Sheets access miss.
- Updated income recent-action rows now display as income and keep the amount/source in the correct fields after source-only updates.
- Large recent-action DM lists are now split into Discord-safe chunks by complete transaction blocks, with controls attached to the final chunk and a generic channel acknowledgement after successful DM delivery.

## Completed 2026-06-18

- Updated expenses can now be moved to another expense category.
- Already moved expenses can now be moved again.
- Deleting an updated expense now deletes the active action lineage, so the original expense does not reappear in recent actions after deletion.
- Deleting a moved expense now deletes the active moved lineage without reactivating stale source actions.
- Undoing those deletes restores the expected sheet rows and action visibility.
- Added regression tests for updated-action move, moved-action move, updated-action delete, and moved-action delete.
- Added explicit recent-action capabilities for update, move, delete, undo, and editable fields.
- Recent-action decision buttons now only show supported operations for the selected transaction.
- Unsupported delete/move/update paths now return clearer reasons for Need expense, payment, savings, and other unsupported cases.
- Income rows can now be updated for source/amount and deleted from recent transactions.
- Added regression tests for capability computation, button visibility, income update, and income deletion.
- Added 300-second TTLs for pending recent-action selections, pending update-field replies, and pending move-item replies.
- Expired pending replies now return a clear "selection expired" message instead of falling through to unrelated commands or current recent-action indexes.
- Added regression tests for expired pending delete selection, expired update-field replies, expired move-item replies, and router numeric replies.
- Recent-action lists, candidate prompts, mutation prompts, and mutation results now send privately to the requesting user when Discord DMs are available.
- Recent-action component responses are now ephemeral, and controls reject interactions from users other than the original actor.
- Added regression tests for private recent-action delivery and non-owner interaction rejection.
- DM replies to recent-action update prompts are now accepted even when BookieBot is restricted to a configured public channel.
- Recent-action component view timeouts now match the 300-second pending-state TTL.
- Added regression tests for DM update-field replies and five-minute component view timeouts.
- Reconciliation digest channel messages now show only a generic count/summary and instruct the target user to review privately.
- Daily reconciliation digest eligibility is now limited to the configured morning send window instead of any time after the send hour.
- Added regression tests proving after-window Plaid/new-item availability does not post a daily digest later in the day.
- Reconciliation digests now send by DM with `Reconcile Now` and `View Inbox` controls.
- Reconciliation `Ignore All` now lives only on the inbox list view, not on each individual transaction review.
- Bills and subscriptions digests now send by DM instead of posting cash-pull details to the shared channel.

## Current Notes

- The broader backlog now lives in `.agent/WORKSTREAM_FINANCE_OPS.md`.
- The task execution/update process lives in `.Agents`.
- Reconciliation freshness and digest lifecycle remain important, but they are not the immediate implementation focus.
- Structured event logging is deferred. Railway/app logs are enough for now unless future debugging gaps prove otherwise.
- We decided not to add a stale status right now; normal unresolved review uses the 60-day freshness filter only.
- The current recent-action tests pass for existing happy paths:
  - listing and paging
  - updating normal expense fields
  - deleting normal expenses with compaction
  - moving normal expenses with compaction
  - undoing update/delete/move

90. Dashboard reimbursement carry-forward: on each phone, verify older unpaid items retain dates and partner grouping; opening/closing entries and received history stays smooth. In a test workbook, settle an older allocation in Discord and refresh: it disappears from Outstanding without changing Income/Spent/Left/Saved. Simulate a ledger read failure and confirm an incomplete-balance message replaces any all-settled claim. Only configured annual workbooks are scanned.

91. Completed dashboard phone acceptance: fully close/reopen BookieBot for this first update; later deployments offer Update now. Select a historical month and Compare spending, return with This month, tap headline labels and category/day drilldowns, and reload to check chart/mode preferences. Ask a report question and follow a source; switching views clears the answer. Under Savings goals, explicitly create a personal goal and record only funds you intend to allocate; contribution history, reversals and archival persist separately from monthly Saved. For notifications, each installed iPhone app independently taps Enable on this phone, allows permission, optionally changes timing/types/amount privacy, then explicitly sends a test; Turn off stops server eligibility. Device delivery requires the owner's actual opt-in and was not sent during automated testing.

92. Mode-switch fix: load the update on each phone with Update now (or fully close/reopen once). Alternate Current/Projected, particularly where Income left or category coverage appears/disappears; repeat in Category Mix All/Needs/Wants/Savings and while another graph is selected. The dashboard must stay visible, and tapping a remaining slice must show that category’s current-mode amount and entries.

93. Dashboard reliability/polish acceptance: choose Update now or close/reopen the Home Screen app. Compare spending starts at Last month; choose another month, switch repeatedly, collapse/reopen, and refresh. Confirm matching date ranges and clear missing-history states. Open/close Category Mix, Calendar, headline and Daily Spending dialogs: background stays stable, Close is centered and only keyboard Tab highlights it. At phone width all five Burn Rate metrics fit, category filters remain at the top, owner sits beside BookieBot and refresh beside month. For each phone, explicitly Enable notifications if desired, confirm On, change time/type/privacy, reopen and verify settings persist. Use Turn off to disable the last type; use Save preferences to retry a failed save. Optional Send a test is the only delivery acceptance action and must be chosen by the owner.

94. Refresh/comparison recovery: load Update now once. Open Compare spending, tap refresh while it is loading, switch comparison month and collapse/reopen. It should settle with matching days and retain Current/Projected. If the connection fails, restore it and tap Try again or refresh; comparison recovers without restarting the app. In a synthetic environment, hold fetch/body promises beyond timeout and confirm Retry becomes available; never use production financial mutations to create failures.

95. Repeated-month comparison acceptance: load Update now on each phone. Open Compare spending and select at least five distinct months (all available months if convenient), then tap Refresh with comparison open and repeat. Values should load without restarting and Current/Projected should persist. In a synthetic/test workbook, edit selected and baseline amounts, refresh, and confirm both comparison sides update. Main refresh always reads fresh data; comparison history can reuse source inputs for up to 60s between refreshes. If Sheets is temporarily busy from other activity, wait about a minute and use Try again; the message should identify Sheets rather than your connection.

96. Notification enable/save recovery: load Update now in each Home Screen app. Choose notification preferences, then tap Enable on this phone again; existing browser permission/subscription can be reused. Verify On and saved confirmation, change delivery time or a toggle, refresh and confirm On plus the saved choices persist. Turn off and re-enable should also succeed. Reinstalling/re-pairing is unnecessary. If desired, explicitly tap Send a test to check real iPhone delivery; no test push is sent automatically.

97. Theme and today-date acceptance: load Update now on each phone. Open the three-dot menu and toggle Dark mode off/on, including taps on the icon and On/Off text. Refresh or reopen and verify the selected theme remains. Today’s Daily Spending number should be bold dark ink on pale green in dark mode, and light on deep green in light mode. Other dates remain unchanged. Keyboard users can activate with Enter/Space, leave the menu with Tab, or dismiss with Escape.

98. Compact comparison acceptance: load Update now. Open Compare spending and confirm the selected range, two totals and amount/percentage change appear without the calculation paragraphs. Details opens/closes smoothly and lists excluded amounts under the matching month names. Change comparison months, refresh, and verify errors still appear directly if a request fails. Both month dropdowns list names/years only; select the current month from the page picker to return to its automatic view. Confirm the masthead reads BookieBot • Brian/Hannah.

99. Compact reimbursement acceptance: load Update now. Shared Reimbursements should show its outstanding balance and up to four proportional expense-month bars, with no expense list or monthly metric statement expanded initially. Bar amounts sum to the balance; Earlier expenses collects older months and Undated stays explicit when present. View expenses reveals partner totals and oldest-first entries; expand an entry to see every original split field. Open the month’s Expense statement for Received/Gross paid/Your share and received history. Try both themes and Current/Projected; the reimbursement balance remains independent of projection. Missing records retain a visible warning and must not suggest everything was repaid.

100. Unified reimbursement acceptance: choose Update now. Confirm the overview has no Across all months / By expense month captions; each bar is labeled with the amount still due for its expense month. Open View expenses: one list groups rows by original month, marks the current month, and shows Received or the remaining amount due directly on each row. Tap a row to see its full item, location/date, split method, responsibility and four split amounts; opening/closing stays smooth. Selected-month Gross paid / Your share / Received totals are inside this same list and exclude older receipts. Browse a historical report month and verify Selected month versus This month labels. In a test workbook only, settle an older expense and refresh: its row remains Received, its outstanding bar/balance decreases, and report Income/Spent/Left/Saved do not change. Check both themes at phone width; incomplete-coverage warnings remain visible.

101. Refresh/history timeout recovery: load Update now. Refresh should retain Current/Projected and the latest successful update time. If history is unavailable, a later successful report refresh should check it once and clear its warning when it recovers; explicit history Retry also remains available. In a synthetic test only, return source_timeout for report/catalog/comparison reads: the app identifies the source timeout, keeps the prior report and usable month options, and never loops on failed attempts. Hold a request beyond the browser deadline and confirm a distinct taking-longer message; restore the source and retry to recover without restarting. No simulated failures or test financial writes should be introduced into production.

102. Reimbursement visual refinement: load Update now, then open View expenses. One segmented bar should fill the entire outstanding total; for $11.99 August + $106.90 September, portions are approximately 10% and 90%, with matching legend colors and exact dollar labels. Month headings have a distinct background; each expense shows its name and Due/Received on one compact line. Tap a row for its date, partner and all split amounts. Tap the selected month’s info button: Gross paid / Your share / Received appear directly beneath that heading and collapse smoothly. Check narrow phone widths and both themes; old received expenses remain visible and incomplete-history warnings remain outside disclosures. No financial writes are needed.

103. Reimbursement receipt styling: load Update now and open View expenses. Month names should read as serif section headings with a thin rule, with no filled heading band. Tap Totals beside the selected month: its chevron rotates and monthly values open/close smoothly. Expand rent or another expense: its name appears only in the summary, with a long name becoming fully readable there. Date/With/optional Location are separated from the prominent Gross paid, paired Your share/Partner share and the Received row; Split method/Expense for are labeled below. Check received, partial and outstanding examples in both themes and at narrow phone width; all amounts stay readable, and the overview bar remains unchanged. No test financial writes are needed.

104. Compact reimbursement accordion: load Update now and open View expenses. Month headings should be smaller and This month should appear in a pill; Totals still opens the month summary. Expand rent: its date/location and Paid by / Split … with … appear together above four aligned label/value rows. Fronted expenses say Fronted for; a different budget owner remains explicit. Open another expense in the same month: the prior row closes while the new one opens smoothly. Open an expense in another month: it remains independent; tapping an open row closes it. Check keyboard, rapid taps, long names/amounts, both themes and 320px width. Overview proportions, every financial amount and monthly totals remain unchanged. No test financial writes are needed.

105. Reimbursement visual A: choose Update now, then Shared Reimbursements → View expenses. Collapsed rows retain the title and plain amount due/Received. Expand an expense: a slim bar shows ivory payer/gross text, blue own share, solid brown partner-paid share and hatched remaining debt; the hatch marker appears beside due in the header. Below it, Yours and the named partner’s paid amount align with matching keys. Verify date/split/location and any fronted/different-budget explanation remain accurate. Check partial, unpaid and received examples already present; opening another row in the same month closes the prior row smoothly. Test both themes and narrow width; monthly Totals and the outstanding overview remain unchanged. Invalid legacy allocations retain five labeled amounts. No test financial writes are needed.

106. Compact Savings goals: choose Update now. All goals should start collapsed with name, exact amount set aside, a thin progress bar, a left chevron and a separate ⋯. Expand one for date/progress/target/remaining; open another and confirm the previous one closes smoothly. Completed goals have a full bar without a checkmark beside their balance, and their optional date remains available. Use ⋯ to inspect History (starting balance plus contribution records), Edit, Record contribution, and Archive; cancellation must leave balances unchanged. Archived goals expose History/Restore through ⋯. New goals open above the list. Check long names/large amounts, both themes and narrow width, keyboard/outside menu dismissal, and no clipped last-row menus. Use a disposable test goal only for actual create/contribute/reverse/archive/restore acceptance; record no test allocations against real goals. Existing uncertain-save Retry this change/Check latest messages remain visible outside disclosures.

107. Lower-dashboard dividers: choose Update now, then scroll from Expense Highlights through Savings goals, Ask BookieBot and Phone notifications. Section and goal-row separator lines should be gone while savings progress bars stay visible. Expand a goal, inspect its History and options, then open Ask BookieBot and notifications; their layout, controls and animation should remain intact. Check both themes and narrow width. No financial changes or notification sends are needed.

108. Savings overview and dates: choose Update now. Savings should use the lighter chart/reimbursement background and show the total recorded across every goal, followed by Goals and the existing compact rows. Archived balances count in the headline and are noted; switching report month does not redefine this total. Open New goal/Edit goal and Record contribution on an actual iPhone: blank and filled date fields must fit like adjacent fields, retain visible focus, and open the native picker. Cancel without changing real goals. Check 320px through desktop, both themes, exact large/fractional totals, and the last goal options menu over the next section. Test actual contributions/reversals/archive/restore only with a disposable goal; totals must follow confirmed balances and remain unchanged on archive/restore. Mobile-emulated desktop WebKit cannot reproduce the iOS-only native padding renderer bug; phone acceptance verifies that platform-specific fix.

109. Confirmed reimbursements: choose Update now on each phone. Brian's Owed to you and Hannah's You owe must show the same obligations; names within item descriptions must not change the payer. Expand the corrected Gameday expense and check payer, gross, original income split and remaining debt. Inspect existing historical received rows without editing them. In isolated test data, record a partial receipt and verify original payer expense decreases by that amount, one debtor expense appears on its payment date, and original gross remains the only bank candidate. Report a sent payment from the debtor: balances stay unchanged until the recipient confirms; pending reports prevent competing receipts/offsets. Review equal allocations before confirming an offset, and verify its reversal restores the entire group. Repeat the same request after a simulated lost response, stale version, session expiry, moved source row and projection failure: no duplicate expenses or double settlement. Check both themes at 320/390/1280px, symmetric disclosures, date containment, one open expense per month and all refresh paths. Never create synthetic transfers or receipts in real financial data.

110. Reimbursement empty-state/motion polish: choose Update now. On You owe with zero balance, see “You don’t currently owe any money.” with no opposite-direction net summary or empty accordion. Owed to you has matching zero-debt wording. Real settled history must still expand if present; pending confirmation, synchronization and retry notices remain available. Toggle View expenses, Compare spending and its Details repeatedly: each + smoothly becomes − and reverses at the same 240ms pace as individual expense rows, with no x/glyph snap or layout movement. Verify 320/390px and desktop, both themes and reduced motion. Cancel any opened payment form; do not submit synthetic financial records. The selected editorial direction tabs show both balances in borderless columns with a short sage underline that slides to the selected direction. Check both choices, long amounts, both themes and reduced motion; no filled card background or outer rule should appear.


111. Four-screen phone app: load the new version, then check Overview, Spending, Shared and Savings on each phone in both themes. The header gear opens Settings and Back returns to the previous tab; theme and notification controls remain usable. On a long screen, scroll down/up to compact/restore the glass bar; returning to a tab restores its position. Check portrait/landscape, iPhone safe areas, actual software keyboard and Reduce Motion. Month and comparison popovers only enable available months; Current/Projected and all four charts remain correct through repeated selections, failed connections and Retry. Open an unsaved savings goal and reimbursement amount form, switch tabs and request another month; both drafts must remain when returning, including while that month is loading or has failed. Cancel these review-only drafts rather than submitting synthetic financial records. Ask from the header, close while a response is pending, reopen to see it, follow a source, and verify month/mode changes clear the interaction. For a later release, × dismisses only that update notice for this session; Settings still offers Update. Update/signout with drafts asks first, while a pending or uncertain real change blocks until resolved. Saved goals, notification settings, signed-in identity and reimbursement history survive the release.

112. Scriptable widget acceptance: update BookieBot, then use Settings → Widgets separately from Brian's and Hannah's signed-in accounts. Remove a connection, then run it online to verify the numbers clear and Reconnect widget appears. Old rendered pixels cannot be erased remotely before iOS runs it again; remove the Home Screen widget to hide them immediately. Re-pair only if desired. No financial writes or notification changes are needed.

114. Widget pairing recovery and Back: update BookieBot, open Settings → Widgets → Set up widget, and use both top/bottom Back buttons plus the gear; edited pairing fields and any existing unexpired setup code should survive, with the previous scroll position restored. Get a setup code should return below the fixed toolbar without creating a connection. Copy script should remain inside BookieBot; if clipboard permission is denied, select/copy the displayed source without leaving the guide. Public /app/widgets/help and /app/widgets/connect pages should both return via Back to Settings, with normal sign-in required if opened in a separate unsigned browser. On iPhone, pair by running the exact BookieBot script inside Scriptable and pasting a new private setup code; verify the owner/figures, then select that same script in Edit Widget with no parameter. To adopt v1.1, replace the existing script code without renaming. Verify unpaired/reconnect/no-data taps open that exact Scriptable script, while populated widgets still open the browser dashboard. Cancel or reject setup and confirm it stops at its prompt/error rather than showing another ambiguous preview. No financial writes or test notification sends are needed.

115. Widget v1.2 authorization recovery: after updating the web app, copy the current script from Settings → Widgets → Set up widget and replace the existing Scriptable source without renaming it; verify v1.2 in the first line. Run inside Scriptable. If pairing was lost, use one new private setup code from the same person’s account; do not reuse a consumed code. Verify the name and amounts, rerun the same script and choose Refresh & preview, then Refresh the BookieBot widget list: Paired becomes Connected with Last checked only after a successful read. Select that same script for both Small and Medium, Parameter blank and When Interacting left at Open App. Both should retain the connection; inspect Home Screen timestamp and tap routing on the actual iPhone. If secure storage reports an error, unlock/retry and retain the exact sanitized error rather than generating repeated connections. Remove obsolete connections only after identifying the working one. No financial writes or test notifications are required.

116. Selectable widget themes: replace the existing Scriptable source with v1.3 from Settings → Widgets → Set up widget, keeping its name and working pairing. Run Theme & preview, choose Editorial then Small; repeat for Two-tone and Medium. Verify the full amounts, name/mode, avatar and one source timestamp fit on the actual iPhone. A negative available amount must stay legible on the sage panel, and a stale preview must retain its source time and Stale label. Set two Home Screen widgets to the same script with Parameters editorial and two-tone; both retain the same account/mode/connection. Clear a Parameter to follow the saved default. Cancel either picker and confirm it leaves the preference unchanged. After a preview, iOS may redraw the Home Screen later. Verify normal widget taps still open the browser dashboard and recovery taps open Scriptable. Native Scriptable sizing/typography and iOS refresh timing cannot be certified by the browser geometry approximation. No new pairing, financial writes or notification sends are required for a working widget.

117. Compact Settings: accept the app update and open Settings on both phone sizes/themes. Appearance, Notifications, Widgets, Account and About should have separate lighter surfaces and open spacing, with no clipped controls. Widgets show compact name/status rows, direct Current/Projected selection, and one Setup & themes link. Open a connection’s ⋯ menu to see its full name/last check and Remove; cancel removal to retain access. Long names should stay within two lines on the row and be readable in options. Setup shows three short steps and initially closed Themes/Help; Copy script stays in place with a selectable fallback, and both Back buttons preserve edited pairing fields or an existing code. After Get a setup code returns to Widgets, verify ⋯ → Remove still opens confirmation on iPhone; outside tap, Escape and rapid toggling leave menus usable. Changes save through existing requests; no new Scriptable installation, pairing, financial writes or notification tests are required to review this design.


118. Widget collection: update BookieBot, then Settings → Widgets → Setup & widgets → Copy script. Replace the existing Scriptable source with v1.4, keeping its name and pairing.
